- `CHATGPT_CODEX_CONTEXT_WINDOW_SETTING` — `const CHATGPT_CODEX_CONTEXT_WINDOW_SETTING: Readonly<{ readonly configKey: "texra.chatgptCodex.contextWindowK"; readonly description: "Input token budget for ChatGPT-subscription (Codex) routing, in thousands of tokens, mirroring Codex CLI's model_context_window. Unset, each model takes the window the Codex backend grants it (500 for GPT-6.1 Sol, 272 for the others); a value overrides it for every model, up to 872 (872,000 tokens). Automatic compaction may run earlier according to the separate compaction threshold. The model's output budget is added when displaying the total context window. The OpenAI backend enforces the real per-account limit — values above what your subscription allows fail and trigger compaction recovery."; readonly max: 872; readonly min: 1; readonly tokensPerUnit: 1000; readonly unitLabel: "K tokens"; }>`
- `CHILD_RUN_CONCURRENCY_BUDGET_CONFIG_KEY` — `const CHILD_RUN_CONCURRENCY_BUDGET_CONFIG_KEY: "texra.childRunConcurrencyBudget"`
- `CHILD_RUN_CONCURRENCY_BUDGET_SETTING` — `const CHILD_RUN_CONCURRENCY_BUDGET_SETTING: Readonly<{ readonly auto: 0; readonly defaultValue: 0; readonly description: "Maximum number of agents one task may run at once in the background; a script's agent() calls also run at most this many at once. 0 (the default) sizes it to this machine's CPU count. Agents beyond the budget wait for a slot to free."; readonly max: 100; readonly min: 0; }>`
